# Satellite `sat-scale` (sat-scale-v1)

Built 2026-11-27T17:45:04Z on Linux 7.0.0-1020-azure x86_64 with zfs-2.4.1-1ubuntu5.1.

A pool at scale: one mirror with 200 filesystems under `fs`, every one of them snapshotted with the rest at each of ten rounds — over 2,000 datasets, 200 filesystems written to a few at a time between the snapshots — for what listing, reading and dating thousands of datasets takes. So many records fill the pool's history, a ring of a thousandth of its space, and it wraps: `zpool history` keeps what the creation logged and, of the rest, the newest alone, and the oldest datasets' coming into being is `zfs get createtxg`'s to say. Its manifests are held out of the regular runs, and its oracle has no `zdb -dddddd` (a file a dataset would be thousands), of `zdb -dddd` each dataset's own line alone, and the datasets of the newest 16 TXGs alone.

## Layout

Ashift 12, as `zpool status` printed it at the end:

```
config:

	NAME                                    STATE     READ WRITE CKSUM
	sat-scale                               ONLINE       0     0     0
	  mirror-0                              ONLINE       0     0     0
	    /var/tmp/sat/members/mirror-0a.img  ONLINE       0     0     0
	    /var/tmp/sat/members/mirror-0b.img  ONLINE       0     0     0

```

## History

`vm/disk-8k` (8K blocks, lz4, written with content it shrinks) and `vm/disk-16k` (16K blocks, sha256, the keystream), 16 MiB of each, and `fs`, twenty text files, twenty of the keystream in a directory, a symbolic and a hard link. Then rounds of four TXGs with 256 KiB written to every volume and a file to `fs` in each, a recursive snapshot `s<round>` after each round. 200 filesystems created under `fs` before the fill; after every round, a file written to twenty of them. Deterministic content from seed `zvolrescue-sat-scale-2026`.

## Oracle

As the golden image's, without older copies: `volumes.sha256`, `zfs-get-destroyed.txt` where something was destroyed, `datasets*.txt`, `datasets-by-txg/`, `labels/`, `labels-uberblocks/`, `zdb-u-export.txt`, `zdb-*.txt` and `zdb-dddddd/`, `files.json`, `zpool-history-il.txt`, `files-capture.txt`, `zpool-status.txt`, `layout.json` (`satellite`: the manifests that name no pool are not run against it), `members.sha256`.
